Previous work has shown that, although NMLNs achieve strong performance as generative models for small relational structures, they underperform diffusion-based generative graph models on larger structures. In this paper, we strengthen NMLNs along two main dimensions: (i) we increase the expressive capacity of their potential functions using graph neural networks, and (ii) we develop a new training and inference algorithm inspired by parallel-tempering Markov chain Monte Carlo methods, which we name parallel noising. Together, these enhancements enable NMLNs to attain strong performance in graph generation relative to general diffusion-based generative graph models. Furthermore, they allow NMLNs to match the performance of specialized text-based recurrent models when generating small molecular structures.

</details>


### [100] [One Model, Many Graphs: Learning over Attributed Graphs across Heterogeneous Modalities with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19128)
*Jiayi Yang,Yifang Chen,Yuanfu Sun,Jiajin Liu,Qiaoyu Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OMG-VLM是一个统一框架，利用预训练视觉-语言模型作为共享骨干网络，结合结构感知图适配器，能够在单一模型中处理纯文本属性、纯图像属性以及多模态属性的异构图，在节点分类和链接预测任务上优于现有GNN和LLM基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图学习方法通常针对固定的模态模式设计，不同模态设置需要单独模型，限制了跨图泛化和可扩展性。属性图存在显著的模态异质性（文本属性、视觉属性、多模态属性），亟需一个统一框架来应对这种多样性。

Method: 以预训练VLM作为共享骨干网络，设计结构感知图适配器，在保持与VLM原生嵌入空间兼容的同时整合邻域信息，实现对文本属性图、图像属性图和多属性图的统一学习。

Result: 在多个领域的实验中，OMG-VLM在节点分类和链接预测任务上一致优于最先进的GNN和LLM基线方法，并展现出对未见图及不同模态模式的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: VLM可作为通用图学习骨干网络，通过结构感知适配器有效处理异构模态属性图，为图结构数据的统一学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) provide a unified representation space for textual and visual information, yet their potential as general-purpose backbones for graph-structured data remains largely unexplored. In practice, attributed graphs exhibit substantial modality heterogeneity: some graphs contain only textual node attributes, others only visual attributes, while still others provide both. Existing graph learning approaches are typically designed for fixed modality schemas, requiring separate models for different settings and limiting scalability and cross-graph generalization. To bridge this gap, we present OMG-VLM (One Model, Many Graphs with Vision-Language Models), a unified framework for learning over attributed graphs across heterogeneous modality schemas. OMG-VLM leverages a pretrained VLM as a shared backbone and introduces structure-aware graph adapters that integrate neighborhood information while remaining compatible with the VLM's native embedding space. This design enables effective learning over text-attributed, image-attributed, and multi-attributed graphs within a single model. Extensive experiments across diverse domains show that OMG-VLM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art GNN- and LLM-based baselines on attributed graph learning tasks such as node classification and link prediction, while exhibiting strong generalization to unseen graphs and varying modality schemas. The source code is available at https://github.com/Jo-eyang/OMG-VLM.

</details>


### [101] [Incomplete Observations Boost Evolutionary Performance in Ocean Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19147)
*Yangyang Kong,Yutong Jiang,Yanhai Gan,Junyu Dong,Feng Gao,Xiaopei Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种生成式状态空间模型与优化框架，通过将海洋物理量视为隐状态、观测数据视为观测变量，利用EM算法和朗之万动力学直接从稀疏、含噪观测中学习海洋动态，实现在无需完整再分析数据集条件下的高保真重建与预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动海洋建模严重依赖完整的再分析数据集，这不仅带来计算约束，且模型性能受限于训练数据质量；真实海洋观测数据稀疏且含噪，亟需一种能够直接从稀疏观测中学习海洋动态的方法。

Method: 构建隐马尔可夫模型，将海洋物理量作为连续隐状态、观测作为观测变量；状态转移和初始状态模块用神经网络实现，发射模块为掩码高斯分布；推导基于EM算法的优化框架，交替使用朗之万动力学重建高保真海洋场并优化深度神经网络捕捉时间演化；假设海洋状态演化满足平稳、遍历、马尔可夫随机过程，仅采用长度-2的状态序列进行优化。

Result: 在CMIP6模拟数据和FY-3D卫星数据上实现高保真重建与准确预测，证明稀疏观测可直接改善模型对海洋状态动力学的表征能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为下一代地球系统模型提供了一条可扩展的路径，使其能够直接从稀疏、不完整的真实观测中学习。

Abstract: Data-driven methods have revolutionized ocean modeling, yet current approaches rely heavily on complete reanalysis datasets, imposing computational constraints and limiting model performance to that of the training data. Here, we present a generative state-space model and an optimization framework that enable learning directly from sparse and noisy observations. The model is essentially a hidden Markov model with a continuous state space, where oceanic physical quantities are treated as hidden states and measurements as observations, enabling a unified representation of ocean fields and observational data. Both the initial-state and state-transition modules are implemented as neural networks to capture the complexity and temporal evolution of ocean states, while the emission module is formulated as a masked Gaussian distribution. To train the model from sparse observations, we derive an optimization framework based on the expectation-maximization (EM) algorithm. The framework alternately reconstructs high-fidelity ocean fields via Langevin dynamics and optimizes deep neural networks to capture temporal evolution. Theoretical analysis shows that the framework maximizes the likelihood of observations under the generative model. For efficiency, we assume that ocean-state evolution follows a stationary, ergodic, and Markovian stochastic process and adopt only length-two state sequences during optimization. Experiments on CMIP6 simulation data and FY-3D satellite data demonstrate high-fidelity reconstruction and accurate prediction, showing that sparse observations can directly improve the model's representation of ocean-state dynamics. This work offers a scalable pathway for next-generation Earth system models to learn directly from sparse, incomplete real-world observations.

</details>


### [102] [Breaking the Homogeneity Assumption: Specialized Multi-Generator Adversarial Learning for Rare Failure Detection in Predictive Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19153)
*Alexis Lazanas,Georgios Kampouropoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对预测性维护中高度不平衡且异构的故障数据，本文提出了一种基于故障类型感知的多生成器GAN增强方法，相比传统重采样方法和单生成器GAN，能生成更真实的少数类样本，在AI4I 2020数据集上取得了更高的PR-AUC和召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统不平衡处理方法（欠采样、SMOTE、代价敏感学习）假设少数类样本是同质的，但工业故障数据中不同故障模式源自不同的物理过程，呈现多模态分布，因此传统方法在多因素工业场景下效果受限。

Method: 提出一种故障类型感知的多生成器GAN架构，每个生成器独立学习一个故障子类型；采用泄漏安全（leakage-safe）的实验设计，比较五种方法：代价敏感学习、随机欠采样、SMOTE过采样、单生成器GAN增强和多生成器GAN增强；使用PR-AUC作为主要评估指标。

Result: 在AI4I 2020预测性维护数据集上，多生成器GAN框架相比传统重采样方法和单生成器GAN，生成了更真实的少数类样本，获得了更高的PR-AUC和召回率。

Conclusion: 故障类型感知的生成式增强方法能有效提升预测性维护系统对罕见故障的识别能力，多生成器架构优于传统不平衡处理方法和单生成器GAN。

Abstract: Supervised learning models in the predictive maintenance field are regularly trained on highly imbalanced industrial datasets: machine failures occur rarely but have a disproportionate effect on operations. In addition to the clear class disparity, failure data are typically non-homogeneous, with different failure modes arising from distinct physical processes and exhibiting a multimodal distribution across minorities and classes. Traditional imbalance-management methods, e.g., undersampling, SMOTE-based interpolation, or cost-sensitive learning, typically assume that the minority population is homogeneous. This means their effectiveness is severely limited in the multifaceted conditions encountered in industrial practice. This paper determines the possibility of a failure-type-conscious generative augmentation program to improve the identification of infrequent failures in predictive maintenance systems. An experimental design that is leakage-safe is used to compare five imbalance-handling methods: cost-sensitive learning, random undersampling, SMOTE oversampling, single-generator GAN augmentation, and a specialized multi-generator GAN architecture that has independent generators that are asked to learn individual failure subtypes. Precision/Recall-oriented measures are used to quantify model performance; the main evaluation measure is the PR-AUC. Experiments conducted on the AI4I 2020 predictive maintenance dataset indicate that the proposed multi-generator GAN framework produces more realistic minority samples, yielding higher PR-AUC and recall scores compared to traditional resampling methods and individual-generator GAN augmentation.

</details>


### [103] [Neural Kolmogorov Equations: Parallelizable Learning of Stochastic Dynamics under General Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19173)
*Arthur Bizzi,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经Kolmogorov方程(NKEs)，通过将神经SDE的学习问题从随机轨迹建模转化为概率密度演化建模，实现确定性无限维重表述，支持广义Lévy型随机强迫学习与并行时间训练，在耦合噪声和跳跃过程等基准上取得具有竞争力的预测精度和更高的训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经SDE方法假设非耦合连续噪声，难以处理实际随机驱动因素，且时间尺度扩展性差，需要昂贵的自回归训练。

Method: 基于Kolmogorov正向方程(KFE)将神经SDE重述为确定性无限维问题；通过KFE的算子结构学习广义Lévy型随机强迫；采用拉格朗日Galerkin投影和算子分裂实现时间并行训练。

Result: 在耦合噪声和跳跃过程等随机基准上，NKEs准确恢复确定性和随机动力学，预测精度具有竞争力，训练效率显著提升。

Conclusion: NKEs为学习随机动力学提供了一种灵活、高效的新范式，克服了传统神经SDE在噪声类型和训练可扩展性方面的关键局限。

Abstract: Neural stochastic differential equations (SDEs) have emerged as powerful tools for learning noisy or stochastic dynamics directly from data; however, existing approaches largely assume uncoupled and continuous noise, limiting their applicability to realistic stochastic drivers, and often scale poorly in time, requiring expensive autoregressive training. To address these limitations, we propose Neural Kolmogorov Equations (NKEs), a deterministic, infinite-dimensional reformulation of Neural SDEs based on the Kolmogorov Forward equation, transforming the learning problem from modelling individual stochastic trajectories to modelling the evolution of probability densities. NKEs learn general Lévy-type stochastic forcing directly through the operator structure of the KFE, and enable parallel-in-time training via a Lagrangian Galerkin projection and operator splitting. We evaluate NKEs on several stochastic benchmarks, including systems with coupled noise and jump processes, and verify that NKEs provide flexible models that accurately recover deterministic and stochastic dynamics with competitive predictive accuracy and improved training efficiency. Code and pretrained models will be released.

</details>


### [104] [Conservative Query and Adaptive Regularization for Offline RL Under Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19199)
*Li-Rong Zhou,Qin-Wen Luo,Sheng-Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CQURE框架，利用不确定性估计来优化离线强化学习中的偏好查询与自适应正则化，在D4RL基准上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线RL中动作偏好查询方法存在两个关键挑战：如何选择信息量丰富的偏好查询，以及如何有效利用收集到的偏好反馈。当前方法仅依赖策略动作与数据集动作之间的距离进行查询选择，并施加固定约束使策略靠近查询偏好，导致策略更新不稳定且与值正则化整合效果差。

Method: 提出Conservative Query and Adaptive Regularization under Uncertainty Estimation (CQURE)框架：1) 使用Morse网络估计策略动作相对于离线数据集的不确定性；2) 基于不确定性提出保守查询策略，仅查询靠近数据集的动作以保持Bellman更新稳定性；3) 提出不确定性感知的自适应正则化方案，在策略优化中动态调整数据级约束。

Result: 将CQURE集成到CQL中，在D4RL基准上进行了广泛评估，实验结果表明在多种任务上取得了优越或具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: CQURE通过不确定性估计同时改进了偏好查询和偏好利用，解决了现有方法中策略更新不稳定和与值正则化整合不良的问题。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) aims to learn an effective policy from a static dataset, but its performance is fundamentally limited by dataset coverage. Action preference queries leverage expert feedback without additional environment interaction, enabling policy improvement during offline training. However, existing methods still face two key challenges: selecting informative preference queries and effectively exploiting the collected feedback. Current approaches typically rely only on the distance between policy actions and dataset actions for query selection, while enforcing fixed constraints that keep the policy close to queried preferences. Such strategies often lead to unstable policy updates and integrate poorly with value regularization. To address these limitations, we propose Conservative Query and Adaptive Regularization under Uncertainty Estimation, a lightweight framework that jointly improves preference querying and preference exploitation. Specifically, we employ a Morse network to estimate the uncertainty of policy actions with respect to the offline dataset. Based on this uncertainty, we introduce a conservative query strategy that selectively queries actions near the dataset to preserve Bellman-update stability, together with an uncertainty-aware adaptive regularization scheme that dynamically adjusts data-level constraints during policy optimization. We integrate our framework with CQL and evaluate it extensively on the D4RL benchmark. Experimental results demonstrate superior or competitive performance across a wide range of tasks.

</details>


### [105] [AdaFlash: Adaptive Speculative Decoding via On-Policy Distilled Diffusion Drafters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19223)
*Yu-Yang Qian,Hao-Cong Wu,Chen Chen,Jiacheng Sun,Zhenhua Dong,Peng Zhao,Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散草稿模型在推测解码中存在的双向注意力导致的高方差问题（领域级和token级），提出AdaFlash框架，包含策略梯度蒸馏（OPD）和自适应长度头，在部署时显著提升加速比和高并发场景下的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散草稿模型（如DFlash）虽能通过并行去噪实现单次前向生成草稿，但其双向注意力机制带来双重问题：领域级的接受率波动剧烈，以及token级的不同位置草稿质量异质性高，导致整体推理效率受限。

Method: 提出AdaFlash框架，包含两个模块：(i) 面向扩散草稿模型的on-policy蒸馏算法，采用reverse-KL散度，实现稳定收敛并降低领域级方差；(ii) 自适应长度头，能够动态调整候选序列长度，降低目标模型的验证开销并处理token级方差。

Result: 实验表明AdaFlash在部署时持续提升加速比，尤其在高峰并发场景中效果显著，相比之前最优方法吞吐量提升最高约66%。

Conclusion: AdaFlash通过针对性设计有效解决了扩散草稿模型中双向注意力带来的双重方差问题，显著提升了推测解码在实际部署中的推理效率。

Abstract: Speculative decoding, in which a lightweight draft model first generates a draft sequence that is then verified in parallel by the target model, has become a prevalent paradigm for accelerating large language model inference. Recent work such as DFlash further boosts drafting efficiency by leveraging diffusion drafters, whose parallel denoising mechanism enables draft generation in a single forward pass. In this work, we uncover a central pitfall of diffusion drafters: bidirectional attention is a double-edged sword. On one hand, it endows the model with parallel generation and global contextual modeling capabilities; on the other hand, this inherent global dependency introduces high variance at both the domain-level and the token-level: acceptance rates fluctuate substantially across different domains, and draft token quality also varies heterogeneously at different token positions. To tackle this issue, we propose AdaFlash framework, comprising two components: (i) an on-policy distillation (OPD) algorithm with reverse-KL divergence tailored for diffusion drafters, bringing stable convergence and effectively reducing domain-level variance; and (ii) an adaptive length head that dynamically adjusts the candidate sequence length on the fly, substantially lowering the verification cost of the target model and handling token-level variance. Experiments demonstrate that AdaFlash consistently improves speedup rate during deployment, with especially significant gains in high-concurrency scenarios, achieving up to approximately 66% higher throughput than previous state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [106] [S3: Stable Subgoal Selection by Constraining Uncertainty of Coarse Dynamics in Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19232)
*Kshitij Kumar Srivastava,Kshitij Jerath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对HRL中高层智能体接收稀疏延迟反馈的问题，本文提出基于粗粒度动力学的内在动机方法，通过混合密度网络建模预测不确定性并生成稠密内在奖励，引导高层智能体进行风险规避的子目标选择，在非平稳长时域任务中超越现有HRL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HRL中高层智能体接收稀疏延迟反馈，且其性能依赖于低层执行能力，导致子目标选择缺乏策略性。现有方法未能有效利用动力学信息为高层提供结构化引导。

Method: 提出基于粗粒度动力学（粗粒度动力学：高层智能体操作时间尺度上多步聚合的环境转移）的内在动机；使用混合密度网络（MDN）评估不同分散度量来建模预测不确定性；将预测不确定性作为稠密内在奖励，引导风险规避的子目标选择。

Result: 动力学感知的稠密内在奖励使高层智能体表现出风险规避的子目标选择行为，在非平稳长时域环境中优于现有HRL方法。

Conclusion: 基于粗粒度动力学的内在动机能够有效稳定高层策略，为导航提供结构化引导，提升HRL在复杂长时域任务中的表现。

Abstract: Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HRL) intends to separate strategic planning from primitive execution. It has been widely successful in solving long-horizon and complex tasks, where flat-RL algorithms have difficulty in learning. However, while the low-level agent in HRL benefits from dense feedback and abundant trial opportunities, the high-level agent receives sparse, delayed feedback from the environment and its performance depends on the low-level execution capability. In this paper, we study whether subgoal selection by the high-level agent can be performed more strategically, by providing it with dynamics-aware intrinsic motivation. Since motivation based on primitive transition dynamics would require broad coverage of the state-action space, we propose to use coarse dynamics, i.e., environment transitions aggregated over multiple steps at the temporal scale at which the high-level agent operates. This approach stabilizes the high-level policy by learning to minimize the predictive uncertainty associated with the coarse dynamics, and provides a guided structure for navigation. We model the predictive uncertainty by evaluating different dispersion metrics as approximated by a Mixture Density Network (MDN). Empirically, we observe that a dense, dynamics-aware intrinsic reward leads to risk-averse subgoal selection, enabling it to outperform state-of-the-art HRL methods in non-stationary long-horizon environments.

</details>


### [107] [In-Context Time Series Classification with Random Convolutional Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19234)
*Joscha Cüppers,Jilles Vreeken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MASHT通过将随机卷积特征（MultiRocket/Hydra）与预训练表格基础模型结合，无需任务特定训练即可实现与SOTA持平的时间序列分类性能，在单变量任务上平均排名甚至优于HIVE-COTE 2.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机卷积变换虽能高效将时间序列映射为表格特征，但通常仅搭配简单线性分类器。作者探究预训练表格基础模型能否更有效地利用这些丰富表示，从而绕过任务特定模型训练。

Method: 提出MASHT流程，将MultiRocket和Hydra特征提取与预训练上下文表格基础模型相结合，仅需特征提取和直接推理，完全跳过任务特定训练。

Result: 单变量任务上平均排名低于HIVE-COTE 2.0，与SOTA基线持平；多变量任务上与最强参考方法高度竞争。

Conclusion: 预训练表格基础模型能有效利用随机卷积特征进行时间序列分类，且无需任务特定训练，为时间序列分析提供了一种高效的新范式。

Abstract: Time series classification is central to domains like medical signal analysis, industrial monitoring, and sensor-based activity recognition, where class information manifests as localized shapes, specific frequencies, temporal shifts, or complex cross-channel interactions. Random convolutional transforms efficiently map these sequences to fixed-dimensional tabular features but are traditionally paired with simple linear classifiers. We investigate whether a pretrained tabular foundation model can more effectively harness these rich representations.
  We propose MASHT, a pipeline that marries MultiRocket and Hydra features with the power of in-context tabular foundation models. By leveraging a pretrained tabular foundation model, our approach completely bypasses task-specific model training, requiring only feature extraction and direct inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MASHT matches state-of-the-art time series classification baselines on univariate tasks, achieving a lower average rank than HIVE-COTE 2.0. On multivariate datasets, MASHT remains highly competitive with the strongest reference methods.

</details>


### [108] [DBMol: Design of High-Affinity, Target-Specific Small Molecules through Structure Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19237)
*Yiming Qin,Kai Yi,Miruna Cretu,Sjors H. W. Scheres,Pietro Liò,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DBMol提出了一种基于结构预测模型（如Boltz-2）引导的从头小分子设计框架，通过交替优化和投影过程（梯度优化+流匹配模型）生成具有高预测亲和力和特异性的化学有效分子，在保持分子多样性的同时显著提升口袋覆盖率，且无需参考配体监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计能与特定蛋白质口袋高亲和力结合的小分子配体是药物发现的核心目标，而近期结构预测模型的突破为下游任务（如结合亲和力预测）提供了基础模型潜力，因此本文旨在利用这些模型进行从头小分子设计。

Method: DBMol采用交替优化与投影过程：优化阶段从初始分子出发，通过梯度优化利用结构预测模型改善口袋特异性相互作用和预测结合亲和力；投影阶段使用流匹配模型将优化的分子图映射为离散且化学有效的分子。

Result: DBMol有效优化了Boltz-2亲和力代理，生成的分子在Boltz-2评估下具有强预测亲和力和特异性；在留出指标（包括AF3评估）下，DBMol显著提升了口袋覆盖率，同时保持了分子多样性，且在无参考配体监督下与无条件生成相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 结构预测模型可作为从头分子设计的有效优化信号，DBMol证明了这一方向的可行性。

Abstract: Designing small molecule ligands that bind with high affinity to specific protein pockets is a fundamental goal in drug discovery, as small molecules constitute a major fraction of approved therapeutics. Recent breakthroughs in structure prediction, such as AlphaFold-3 and Boltz-2, enable accurate biomolecular interaction prediction and show promise as foundation models for downstream tasks, including binding affinity prediction. We propose to leverage these models and introduce DBMol, a new structure predictor-guided framework for de novo small molecule design. DBMol formulates an alternating optimization and projection process. In the optimization stage, DBMol starts from an initial molecule and uses gradient-based optimization to improve pocket-specific interactions and predicted binding affinity using a structure prediction model. In the projection stage, a flow-matching model maps the optimized molecular graph to discrete and chemically valid molecules. Experiments show that DBMol effectively optimizes the Boltz-2 affinity proxy and generates molecules with strong predicted affinity and specificity under Boltz-2 evaluation. To reduce self-confirmation bias, we further evaluate generated molecules using held-out metrics, including AF3-based evaluation. DBMol substantially improves pocket coverage while maintaining molecular diversity over unconditional generation, and is competitive under held-out metrics despite the absence of reference-ligand supervision. These results support the promise of structure prediction models as effective optimization signals for de novo molecular design.

</details>


### [109] [Thermodynamics-Informed Input Reparameterization for Neural Prediction of Real-Fluid Thermodynamic Properties in Supercritical Combustion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19241)
*Haoze Zhang,Han Li,Ke Xiao,Yangchen Xu,Runze Mao,Zhi X. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种热力学知识引导的输入重参数化策略（TAIR），通过用目标匹配的热力学坐标替代原始焓坐标，显著提升了超临界燃烧模拟中神经网络代理模型对温度、密度和可压缩系数的预测精度，RMSE降低1.5-7.5倍，外推场景下提升更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超临界燃烧模拟中，基于焓的压力修正格式需要反复进行真实流体热力学性质评估（温度T、密度ρ、可压缩系数ψ），计算成本高昂。尽管神经网络代理模型可提供固定成本推理，但直接从(h,p,Y)映射到(T,ρ,ψ)需要同时捕捉焓-温度关系和非理想状态方程响应，回归问题复杂，精度受限。

Method: 提出目标对齐输入重参数化（TAIR）：对温度网络，用常c_p理想气体混合物焓近似反演得到的温度估计替代原始焓坐标；对密度和可压缩系数网络，用理想气体密度估计替代原始焓坐标。这些代数变换仅使用求解器可用变量和物种常数，引导网络学习真实流体相对于理想气体基线的偏离量，而非从原始焓重建完整闭合关系。

Result: 在超临界甲烷-氧对冲火焰数据上，TAIR相比原始输入基线，对T、ρ、ψ的保留集RMSE分别降低约1.5、2.0、7.5倍；在未见应变率火焰（热力学包络扩展场景）中，对应倍数为3.6、14.5、6.0。目标不一致的交叉重参数化控制实验表现更差，验证了增益源于热力学匹配的输入设计而非通用预处理。

Conclusion: TAIR通过热力学知识引导的输入重参数化，有效降低了神经网络学习复杂真实流体闭合关系的难度，显著提升了预测精度和外推能力，为超临界燃烧模拟中高效准确的热力学性质评估提供了新思路。

Abstract: Real-fluid thermodynamic property evaluation is a major computational cost in supercritical combustion simulations. In the enthalpy-based pressure-correction formulation, the closure evaluates temperature T, density $ρ$, and compressibility coefficient $ψ$ from the solver state (h,p,Y) through enthalpy-temperature inversion and repeated real-fluid equation-of-state evaluations. Neural-network surrogates offer fixed-cost inference, but direct mapping from (h,p,Y) to $(T,ρ,ψ)$ must capture the enthalpy-temperature relation and non-ideal equation-of-state response, resulting in a complex regression problem. This work introduces a thermodynamics-informed input reparameterization strategy, termed target-aligned input reparameterization (TAIR). TAIR replaces the raw enthalpy coordinate of each property network with a target-matched thermodynamic coordinate: the temperature network uses a temperature estimate obtained by inverting a constant-$c_p$ ideal-gas mixture enthalpy approximation, whereas the density and compressibility networks use an ideal-gas density estimate. These algebraic transformations use only solver-available variables and species constants, guiding the networks to learn real-fluid departures from ideal-gas baselines rather than reconstructing the full closure from raw enthalpy. The method is assessed using supercritical methane-oxygen counterflow flame data against a raw-input baseline and target-inconsistent cross-reparameterization controls. TAIR reduces held-out RMSE by factors of about 1.5, 2.0, and 7.5 for T, $ρ$, and $ψ$, respectively. For an unseen strain-rate flame within the augmented thermodynamic envelope, the corresponding factors are 3.6, 14.5, and 6.0. The target-inconsistent controls perform worse, indicating that the gains arise from thermodynamically matched input design rather than generic preprocessing.

</details>


### [110] [Benchmarking Generalization in Financial Statement Fraud Detection: robust evaluation and novel tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19259)
*Guy Stephane Waffo Dzuyo,Gaël Guibon,Christophe Cerisara,Luis Belmar-Letelier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型的财务报告舞弊检测框架，通过整合结构化和非结构化文本数据，并构建了公司隔离的基准任务CI-FSFD以实现更真实的泛化性能评估，在挑战性任务上取得了最优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有财务舞弊检测方法依赖随机数据划分，导致性能估计过于乐观，无法反映对新公司或未来时期的真实泛化能力；同时，财务报告中的文本数据未被充分利用。

Method: 提出基于大语言模型的鲁棒FSFD框架，融合结构化财务数据与非结构化文本信息；构建公司隔离的基准任务CI-FSFD进行更现实的评估；创建并公开了包含财务报表、MD&A文本摘要和舞弊标签的美国公司综合数据集。

Result: 该方法在具有挑战性的CI-FSFD任务上取得了最佳性能。

Conclusion: 文本数据与鲁棒评估对于可靠的财务舞弊检测具有关键价值。

Abstract: Financial statement fraud detection (FSFD) is crucial for market integrity but faces challenges from increasingly sophisticated schemes and under-utilized textual data in financial reports. Existing methods often rely on random data splits, leading to overoptimistic performance estimates that do not reflect real-world generalization to new companies or future periods. To address this recurring problem with the state of the art, we propose a robust FSFD framework leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to integrate both structured financial data and unstructured textual information from financial reports. We provide a more realistic evaluation through a novel and challenging benchmark task called Company-Isolated FSFD (CI-FSFD). We construct and make publicly available a comprehensive U.S. company dataset combining financial statements, summarized MD&A text, and fraud labels. Our approach achieves the best performance on the challenging CI-FSFD task, demonstrating the critical value of textual data and robust evaluation for reliable financial fraud detection.

</details>


### [111] [Toward Auditable Fraud Detection: Combining Graph Features, Model Explanations, and Agentic Case Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19266)
*Rahil Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在PaySim数据集上研究分层欺诈检测管线，发现图特征和异常检测信号在完整测试集上无提升，但能改进中间分数样本的排序；在注入的多账户欺诈环中，结构特征可恢复所有欺诈交易，而基模型遗漏约四分之一；LLM调查代理性能低于直接阈值分类器，其更改的8个决策中有6个将正确输出改为错误，但探索性分歧升级规则能识别其中2个错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺诈检测系统需要随交易量扩展，同时保持可解释性和可审查性，因此研究分层管线中各组件（梯度提升分类器、图特征、自编码器异常信号、TreeSHAP解释、LLM调查代理）在特定条件下的贡献。

Method: 在PaySim数据集上构建分层管线：梯度提升分类器基线、图结构特征、自编码器异常信号、TreeSHAP解释，以及针对分类器不确定样本的LLM调查代理。先识别并移除模拟器特有的余额捷径，再进行实验比较。

Result: 图特征和异常信号在全测试集上无AP提升，但改进中间分数样本排序；结构特征在注入多账户欺诈环中恢复所有欺诈交易；LLM调查代理准确率65.0%低于直接阈值71.7%，其更改的8个决策中6个引入错误。

Conclusion: 分层欺诈系统中各组件仅在特定条件下有贡献，LLM调查代理的合理解释并非更优决策的证据，分歧升级规则可辅助识别代理错误。

Abstract: Fraud detection systems must scale with rising transaction volume while remaining explainable and reviewable. We study a layered pipeline on the PaySim dataset that combines a gradient-boosted classifier, graph-derived structural features, an autoencoder-based anomaly signal, TreeSHAP explanations, and a bounded LLM investigation agent applied to cases the classifier scores uncertainly. Before any model comparison, we identify and remove a simulator-specific balance shortcut that would otherwise inflate baseline performance. After this correction, neither the graph features nor the anomaly signal improves Average Precision on the full test set. Both, however, rank fraud better within the subset of cases receiving intermediate baseline scores. In a controlled experiment with injected multi-account fraud rings, engineered structural features recover all injected test transactions, while the tabular baseline misses roughly a quarter of them. The investigation agent underperforms direct thresholding of the classifier it relies on, reaching 65.0% accuracy against 71.7% on a balanced 60-case sample, despite having access to model explanations, graph context, and retrieved reference cases. Of the eight decisions the agent changed, six replaced correct classifier outputs with errors, and it produced a coherent written rationale in each case. An exploratory disagreement-based escalation rule flagged two of these agent errors for human review without flagging any correct decision. We conclude that each component of a layered fraud system contributes only under specific conditions, and that a plausible rationale from an investigation agent is not evidence of a better decision.

</details>


### [112] [GUIDED Network-Agnostic Feature Initialization for Spatial Transferability in GNN-based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19270)
*Alessandro Scalese,Santhanakrishnan Narayanan,Constantinos Antoniou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GUIDED初始化层，将交通需求作为辅助虚拟链路标量属性注入，解决了图神经网络在交通分配问题中的空间泛化瓶颈，使模型能够在不同城市拓扑间无缝迁移，同时保持高精度并降低训练时间约50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图神经网络作为交通分配问题的数据驱动替代方案，存在严重的空间泛化缺口：标准模型的转导特征初始化将出行需求绑定在固定网络拓扑上，无法无缝迁移到新的城市环境，限制了实际部署。

Method: 提出GUIDED（几何无约束归纳需求嵌入）层，一种网络无关的初始化层，将出行需求作为标量属性注入辅助虚拟链路，而非作为特定的节点特征，从而标准化不同规模网络的输入空间。与异构图注意力网络（HetGAT）集成使用。

Result: 在单网络任务上保持最先进的预测精度，对分布外需求模式展现出更强的鲁棒性，在数据稀缺条件下保持明显性能优势；支持跨网络迁移学习的参数高效域适应；每轮训练时间相比基线减少约50%。

Conclusion: GUIDED为真正的归纳模型奠定了坚实基础，其空间拓扑的抽象化为广义起讫点空间问题（如货运物流、多模态网络优化）提供了通用蓝图。

Abstract: The Traffic Assignment Problem is a fundamental but computationally expensive component of transportation planning. While Graph Neural Networks have emerged as fast, data-driven surrogates, their practical deployment is severely constrained by a spatial generalization gap. Standard models rely on transductive feature initializations that tie travel demand to fixed network topologies, preventing seamless transfer to new urban environments. To overcome this structural limitation, this research proposes a network-agnostic initialization layer, termed Geometrically Unconstrained Inductive Demand EmbeDding (GUIDED). By injecting travel demand as a scalar attribute on auxiliary virtual links rather than as specific node features, this modular framework standardizes the input space regardless of network scale. Extensive experimental evaluation across multiple urban topologies demonstrates that a Heterogeneous Graph Attention Network (HetGAT) model integrated with the proposed GUIDED layer maintains state-of-the-art predictive accuracy on single-network tasks, while demonstrating superior robustness to out-of-distribution demand patterns and maintaining a distinct performance advantage over the baseline even under severe data scarcity. Notably, the proposed feature initialization enables highly parameter-efficient domain adaptation for inter-network transfer learning without artificial input homogenization, establishing a robust foundation for truly inductive models. At the same time, the optimized scatter operations of the initialization layer yield an approximate 50% reduction in training time per epoch compared to the baseline approach. Furthermore, while demonstrated on vehicular traffic, this fundamental abstraction of spatial topology provides a versatile blueprint for generalized origin-destination spatial problems, such as freight logistics and multimodal network optimization.

</details>


### [113] [A Reinforcement-Learning-Augmented Liquid-Fueled Reactor Network Model for Predicting Lean Blowout in Gas Turbine Combustors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19281)
*Philip John,Eloghosa Ikponmwoba,Pinaki Pal,Opeoluwa Owoyele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于强化学习的框架，用于生成最优液体燃料反应器，以改善燃气轮机燃烧室中的贫油吹熄（LBO）预测。该框架采用多阶段聚类-分类策略，先通过初始聚类生成大量均匀微簇，再由actor-critic RL智能体将其合并为最优反应区。在Jet-A机制上的验证表明，该方法相比k-means聚类具有更好的预测精度，能捕捉正确的LBO趋势，同时相比高保真计算模型实现了显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有确定簇边界的方法依赖人工启发式或基于输入空间的距离度量，缺乏对目标指标（如LBO预测精度）的直接考量，导致预测性能受限。

Method: 采用多阶段聚类-分类策略：1) 初始聚类（如k-means）生成大量同质微簇；2) actor-critic RL智能体以目标导向方式将微簇合并为最优反应区，显式优化LBO预测精度。

Result: 在Jet-A机理（119种物种、841个反应）上的验证表明：RL框架相比k-means聚类具有更好的预测保真度，能正确捕捉LBO趋势，同时相比高保真计算模型实现了显著加速。

Conclusion: RL驱动的优化方法作为一种计算高效的降阶建模技术，能够补充高保真模拟，用于快速设计空间探索，具有很大潜力。

Abstract: This study introduces a reinforcement learning (RL) framework for generating optimal liquid-fueled reactors to improve lean blowout (LBO) predictions in gas turbine combustors. Existing approaches for determining cluster boundaries rely on manual heuristics or distance-based metrics in the input space. In contrast, the proposed method is goal-oriented, explicitly accounting for the target metric (e.g., LBO prediction accuracy) during cluster formation. The framework employs a multi-stage clustering--classification strategy: an initial clustering step (e.g., $k$-means clustering) generates a large set of homogeneous micro-clusters, followed by an actor-critic RL agent that merges them into optimal reactor zones. The validation study, performed using a Jet-A mechanism (119 species, 841 reactions), shows the RL framework offers improved predictive fidelity compared to $k$-means and captures the correct LBO trends, while achieving substantial speedups relative to the high-fidelity computational model. Overall, the RL-driven approach demonstrates strong potential as a computationally efficient reduced-order modeling technique that can complement high-fidelity simulations for rapid design-space exploration.

</details>


### [114] [Real-time optimal control with shallow recurrent decoder networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19302)
*Matteo Tomasetto,Francesco Braghin,J. Nathan Kutz,Andrea Manzoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用SHRED-ROM（基于浅循环解码器网络的降阶模型）从少量传感器读数中合成实时闭环控制器，用于高维参数化动力学系统的自适应控制，通过模仿专家示教器的最优控制示例，有效缓解了维数灾难，并在三个高维案例中验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最优控制问题在应对多场景高维时空动力学系统时，通常需要大量系统仿真，计算开销巨大。亟需一种能够在实时条件下仅依赖有限传感器信息即可实现多场景自适应控制的高效方法。

Method: 采用SHRED-ROM（浅循环解码器网络降阶模型）从少量传感器读数中学习潜在动力学；通过模仿学习专家示教器提供的最优控制示例；合成传感器预测器在潜在空间层面闭环控制，以应对传感器故障或延迟。

Result: 在参数化密度控制和流体流动控制三个高维挑战性案例中验证了所提策略的有效性，实现了实时控制且有效缓解了维数灾难。

Conclusion: SHRED-ROM能够仅依赖有限传感器读数合成实时闭环控制器，高效模仿专家控制行为，并在新场景中实现有效的分布式控制，具有处理高维参数化动力学系统的潜力。

Abstract: Controlling dynamical systems in real-time across multiple scenarios is critical to enabling adaptive control strategies, ensuring stability and efficiency. However, to tailor control actions in response to varying scenarios, traditional optimal control problems typically require several system simulations, which are often computationally demanding due to the high-dimensionality of the underlying spatio-temporal dynamics. In this work, we exploit SHallow REcurrent Decoder networks-based Reduced Order Modeling (SHRED-ROM) to synthesize a real-time closed-loop controller for high-dimensional and parametric dynamics, relying solely on limited state sensor readings. After training the model on a few optimal examples given by an expert demonstrator, SHRED-ROM mimics the expert behavior with effective distributed control actions in new scenarios, alleviating the curse of dimensionality. Moreover, a sensor forecaster is synthesized and used to close the loop at the latent level, thus efficiently mitigating possible sensor failures or delays. The performance of the proposed optimal control strategy is finally assessed on three challenging high-dimensional cases dealing with either parametric density control or fluid flow control.

</details>


### [115] [Riemannian Deep Learning:Modules, Networks, and Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19305)
*Chen Ziheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一个统一的黎曼深度学习框架，从可重用神经模块、流形特定网络架构和底层几何设计三个互补视角出发，将批归一化推广到李群和陀螺群，将多项式逻辑回归扩展到SPD流形和一般黎曼流形，开发了双曲空间无约束模型、Busemann双曲学习、满秩相关矩阵等几何表示的神经网络，并引入了SPD流形上可学习且计算高效的黎曼度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度神经网络在处理流形值表示时，许多基本组件仍局限于特定流形、依赖欧几里得近似，或需要昂贵且数值不稳定的几何操作，缺乏统一的黎曼深度学习框架。

Method: 论文从三个互补视角构建统一框架：1) 将批归一化从欧氏空间推广到李群和陀螺群；2) 将多项式逻辑回归从欧氏空间扩展到SPD流形再到一般黎曼流形；3) 开发双曲空间无约束模型、Busemann双曲学习、满秩相关矩阵等特定几何表示的神经网络；4) 引入SPD流形上可学习Log-Euclidean度量和快速稳定的Cholesky度量。

Result: 通过理论分析和在视觉、信号处理、图学习、基因组学等领域的数值实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该统一框架解决了黎曼深度学习中模块泛化性、数值稳定性和计算效率等关键问题，为流形值表示的深度学习提供了系统性的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep neural networks on manifold-valued representations have attracted growing interest, but many basic components remain tied to specific manifolds, rely on Euclidean approximations, or require costly and numerically fragile geometric operations. This thesis develops a unified framework for Riemannian deep learning from three complementary perspectives: reusable neural modules, manifold-specific network architectures, and the design of underlying geometries. It generalizes batch normalization from Euclidean spaces and individual manifolds to broad classes of Lie groups and gyrogroups, and extends multinomial logistic regression from Euclidean space to SPD manifolds and then to general Riemannian manifolds. It further develops neural networks for several important geometric representations, including an unconstrained model of hyperbolic space, Busemann-based hyperbolic learning, and full-rank correlation matrices. Finally, it introduces adaptive and computationally efficient Riemannian metrics on SPD manifolds, including learnable Log-Euclidean geometries and fast, stable Cholesky-based geometries. The proposed methods are supported by theoretical analysis and validated through numerical experiments and applications in vision, signal processing, graph learning, and genomics.

</details>


### [116] [Staypoint Detection from Noisy Trajectory Data [Experiment Paper]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19312)
*Lance Kennedy,Hossein Amiri,Yueyang Liu,Riyang Bao,Hanqi Chen,Mohammad Hashemi,Ruochen Kong,Xiaotong Liu,Joon-Seok Kim,Shengpu Tang,Liang Zhao,Andreas Züfle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为停留点检测任务构建了标准基准：发布了16个大规模模拟数据集（含数千个代理的真实停留点标注），并系统评估了9种检测算法。结果显示现有SOTA方法在真实噪声条件下表现不佳，而本文提出的无监督方法有显著改进，监督方法则大幅超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 停留点检测是轨迹数据语义分析的基础，但长期以来缺乏标准基准和系统评估，主要原因是缺少同时包含原始个体轨迹和真实停留点标注的公开数据集。

Method: (1) 构建16个大规模模拟数据集，包含数千个代理及其标注停留点，并引入不同级别的轨迹噪声；(2) 评估9种停留点检测算法（包括现有SOTA和新提出的方法），分析它们对噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 现有SOTA算法在真实噪声条件下表现较差；本文提出的无监督方法有显著改进；监督方法大幅超越现有基线。

Conclusion: 这些数据集和方法仅作为未来停留点检测研究的起点，领域仍有进一步探索空间。

Abstract: Detecting staypoints from raw trajectory data is fundamental to numerous spatial computing applications. This process transforms raw numeric sequences of geolocations into semantically meaningful locations, such as homes, workplaces, or restaurants. Despite its importance for semantic trajectory analysis, staypoint detection lacks standard benchmarks, and existing algorithms have never been systematically evaluated. This gap persists because no publicly available datasets provide both raw individual trajectories and ground-truth staypoint annotations. This benchmark paper addresses this limitation with two key contributions: (1) we introduce 16 large-scale simulated datasets capturing thousands of agents with annotated staypoints across varying trajectory noise levels, and (2) we evaluate nine staypoint detection algorithms-including both state-of-the-art and novel methods-to analyze their robustness to noise. Our evaluation reveals that existing state-of-the-art algorithms perform poorly under realistic noise conditions. Conversely, our proposed unsupervised methods yield substantial improvements, while supervised approaches drastically outperform existing baselines. While these results are very promising, these datasets and methods are only meant as starting points for future research in staypoint detection.

</details>


### [117] [Off-Context GRPO: Learning to Reason on Hard Problems using Privileged Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19313)
*Priyank Agrawal,Ankur Samanta,Shervin Ghasemlou,Jalaj Bhandari,Kavosh Asadi,Daniel Jiang,Aditya Modi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Off-Context GRPO (OC-GRPO)，通过在训练中使用带特权引导的解前缀生成轨迹，并引入重要性校正目标来避免与原始无引导目标的偏差，从而在数学推理任务上比标准GRPO提升3.9%的绝对性能（13.8%相对提升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLVR方法在模型无法生成任何正确解时会收到零奖励信号，导致学习停滞。为了解决这一"学习悬崖"问题，需要提供特权引导（如解前缀）来帮助模型获得非零奖励信号。

Method: 提出OC-GRPO，在GRPO基础上引入带特权引导的off-context轨迹生成，并应用重要性校正目标（importance-corrected objective），确保更新方向回归到原始无引导目标，避免引导训练中的分布不匹配问题。

Result: 在标准数学推理基准上，OC-GRPO相比标准GRPO实现了3.9%的绝对提升（13.8%相对提升），且额外计算成本可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 通过提供特权引导并结合重要性校正，OC-GRPO有效克服了困难问题上的零奖励学习信号问题，以极小代价显著提升了数学推理性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves reasoning in large language models. Yet, typical RLVR approaches fail on difficult problems: when a model cannot generate any correct solutions, it receives \textit{zero} learning signal. Providing privileged guidance during training, such as solution prefixes, can help overcome this learning cliff by steering the model towards {correct solutions with non-zero reward}. {We call these rollouts \textit{off-context}: they are generated from a training prompt that contains privileged guidance, while the target objective is defined by the original prompt without that guidance.} {We introduce} Off-Context GRPO (OC-GRPO), a minimally modified variant of GRPO that uses guided rollouts but applies an importance-corrected objective to steer the update back toward the original unguided objective, avoiding the mismatch that destabilizes uncorrected guided training. Empirically, our algorithm achieves a 3.9\% absolute improvement (13.8\% relative gain) over vanilla GRPO on average across standard mathematical reasoning benchmarks with negligible additional cost.

</details>


### [118] [CircuitKIT : Circuit Discovery, Evaluation, and Application Toolkit for Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19317)
*Pratinav Seth,Hem Gosalia,Aditya Kasliwal,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CircuitKIT 是一个连接电路分析工作流的开源库，提供类型化、可序列化的表示，包含发现算法、声明式接口、诊断工具和下游应用模块，旨在统一和简化电路分析流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前电路分析工作流碎片化严重，发现、评估和干预环节需要拼接不同的独立实现，且对比分析所需的提示需要手动编写，导致方法难以比较，应用范围受限。

Method: CircuitKIT 通过类型化、可序列化的表示连接电路分析工作流，提供一套发现算法、将结构化数据映射到发现任务的声明式接口、补充性电路诊断工具以及下游应用模块。

Result: 提供了一个统一的公共基础设施，支持电路分析的开展和比较，并配套发布了库、示例、笔记本和文档。

Conclusion: CircuitKIT 通过统一的类型化表示和模块化组件，有效解决了电路分析工作流碎片化问题，降低了方法比较的门槛，拓展了电路分析在剪枝、编辑、引导和选择性微调等下游干预任务中的应用。

Abstract: Circuit analysis can support not only model explanation but also downstream interventions such as pruning, editing, steering, and selective fine-tuning. However, conducting such analyses currently requires stitching together separate implementations for discovery, evaluation, and intervention, as well as hand-authoring the contrastive prompts required by many discovery methods. This fragmentation makes methods difficult to compare and limits their application beyond canonical tasks. We introduce CircuitKIT, a source-available library that connects the circuit-analysis workflow through a typed, serializable representation. CircuitKIT provides a suite of discovery algorithms, declarative interfaces for mapping structured data into discovery tasks, complementary circuit diagnostics, and downstream application modules. Together, these components provide common infrastructure for conducting and comparing circuit analyses. The library, examples, notebooks, and documentation are released at https://github.com/Lexsi-Labs/CircuitKIT .

</details>


### [119] [ISO: An RLVR-Native Optimization Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19331)
*Hanqing Zhu,Wenyan Cong,Zhizhou Sha,Sagnik Mukherjee,Xinyuan Song,David González-Martínez,Xiaoxia Wu,Yuandong Tian,Shiwei Liu,David Z. Pan,Zhangyang "Atlas" Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出等谱优化(ISO)框架，揭示强化学习与可验证奖励(RLVR)训练中的谱继承现象：基础模型的权重谱在训练中被保留，仅输入输出奇异帧发生变化。ISO包括离线合并(ISO-Merger)和在线优化(ISO-Optimizer)两种实现，在推理和编程任务上显著提升训练效率，例如ISO-AdamW仅用100步即达到AdamW 270步的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽能提升语言模型推理能力，但其将奖励反馈转化为权重更新的优化层机制尚未被充分理解。作者希望探索这一缺失的优化层，理解模型权重在RLVR训练中的结构变化规律。

Method: 通过奇异值分解分析模型权重结构，提出谱继承假说并设计等谱优化(ISO)框架。离线版本ISO-Merger通过合并共享基座专家的帧变化实现无数据融合；在线版本ISO-Optimizer固定基座谱，仅优化帧变量，可兼容AdamW和Muon等优化器。

Result: 在1.5B至8B参数的推理和编程任务上，ISO-Optimizer在更少训练步数下达到或超越基线性能。Qwen3-8B-Base上，AdamW需270步达0.495准确率，ISO-AdamW仅100步即达相同水平，210步进一步提升至0.509。

Conclusion: RLVR后训练不应全盘继承预训练优化策略，而应围绕奖励驱动适应的结构特性设计：继承谱，优化帧。ISO为RLVR缺失的优化层提供了具体答案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is rapidly advancing the reasoning capabilities of language models, yet the optimization layer that converts reward feedback into weight-space updates remains poorly understood. Building on our prior analysis (Zhu et al., 2025), we study this missing layer through the singular structure of model weights and identify spectral inheritance: RLVR can reuse the base model's weight spectra while acquiring new behavior through changes in the associated input and output singular frames.
  We operationalize spectral inheritance as Isospectral Optimization (ISO), an RLVR-native, fixed-spectrum optimization framework with complementary offline and online instantiations. Offline, ISO-Merger combines the frame changes of shared-base specialists into a single fixed-spectrum model, requiring no post-merge data, rollouts, gradient updates, or on-policy distillation (OPD). It recovers complementary specialist capabilities and achieves the strongest aggregate performance among the compared data-free merging methods. Online, ISO-Optimizer applies a chosen base optimizer, including AdamW and Muon, to the frame variables while keeping the base spectra fixed. Across reasoning and coding tasks ranging from 1.5B to 8B parameters, ISO-Optimizer improves accuracy in the reported runs and reaches matched scores with substantially fewer training steps. On Qwen3-8B-Base, AdamW reaches an aggregate accuracy of 0.495 after 270 training steps. ISO-AdamW reaches the same accuracy after only 100 training steps and improves further to 0.509 after 210 training steps. Together, ISO offers a concrete answer to RLVR's missing optimization layer: rather than inheriting pre-training optimization wholesale, design post-training around the structure of reward-driven adaptation: inherit the spectrum, optimize the frames.

</details>


### [120] [ROMS-IMLE: A Minimalist Approach to Competitive Single-Step Generative Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19332)
*Chirag Vashist,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑了扩散模型需要多步迭代去噪的必要性，提出使用IMLE目标函数和简单卷积网络的单步生成模型，在ImageNet 256上达到FID 2.56，兼具高精度和召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生成模型中逐步将噪声分布变换为数据分布（如扩散模型和流匹配）是否真正必要，挑战这一普遍信念，追求极简设计。

Method: 采用IMLE（隐式最大似然估计）作为训练目标，摒弃变分推理、对抗训练和数值积分等复杂方法；模型使用中等规模卷积网络而非Transformer，仅添加真正必要的元素，不包含迭代去噪过程。

Result: 在ImageNet 256x256上实现FID 2.56，同时保持良好精度和召回率，是一个单步、参数高效的生成模型。

Conclusion: 逐步迭代去噪并非高性能生成模型的必要条件，通过极简设计——简单目标函数（IMLE）和简单模型（卷积网络）——即可达到有竞争力的生成质量。

Abstract: Generative models have undergone many generations of evolution, from VAEs/GANs to diffusion/flow matching. Along the way, the underlying techniques have become more complicated and various beliefs about what drives strong empirical performance have taken hold. Due to the success of diffusion models and flow matching, one of the more common beliefs is the importance of transforming the noise distribution to the data distribution gradually through many small transformations. We ask whether this is truly necessary, and take a minimalist approach to designing a competitive generative model. We start with the bare-bones essentials, namely just a training objective and a model. We purposefully make both simple. For the training objective, we choose Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation (IMLE), and eschew more complicated alternatives such as variational inference, adversarial training and numerical integration. For the model, we eschew transformers and instead choose a moderately sized convolutional network. Then we judiciously added elements that are truly essential, which surprisingly do not include iterative denoising. The result is a single-step parameter-efficient generative model that produces high quality samples at fast speed: it achieves an FID of 2.56 on ImageNet 256 and simultaneously attains good precision and recall.

</details>


### [121] [Provable diffusion-based posterior sampling for linear inverse problems via DDIM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19333)
*Yuchen Jiao,Na Li,Changxiao Cai,Yuxin Chen,Gen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PDDIM算法，通过对DDIM更新进行轻量级、坐标维度的修改，并显式融入测量模型，实现线性逆问题的后验采样。该方法沿测量算子的每个奇异方向分别进行后验采样，根据观测信噪比与扩散信噪比的比较自适应切换先验驱动和测量驱动。理论上证明了算法收敛到贝叶斯后验，实验上在多项图像恢复任务中取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散模型的逆问题后验采样方法要么缺乏严格的理论保证，要么计算开销过大，作者旨在提出一种既高效又有严格理论保证的算法。

Method: PDDIM算法：对标准DDIM更新进行坐标维度的轻量修改，沿测量算子的每个奇异方向分别进行后验采样。当观测信噪比低于扩散信噪比时遵循学习到的扩散先验，否则切换为基于测量的校准预测器。

Result: 理论证明了算法收敛到以测量为条件的贝叶斯后验；实验上在多项图像恢复任务中优于现有扩散后验采样器，在大多数评估指标上取得了最佳性能。

Conclusion: 将含噪线性逆问题的后验采样转化为简单的坐标维度DDIM更新，得到了一个高效、易于实现且具有后验一致性的算法。

Abstract: Diffusion-based methods have achieved remarkable empirical success in solving inverse problems. However, many existing posterior samplers either lack rigorous theoretical guarantees or incur substantial computational overhead. We propose a simple and efficient algorithm, called \pddim, for solving linear inverse problems with diffusion priors via a DDIM-type sampler. Our method requires only lightweight, coordinate-wise modifications to the standard DDIM update, while explicitly incorporating the measurement model. The key idea is to perform posterior sampling separately along each singular direction of the measurement operator: for each direction, the sampler follows the learned diffusion prior when the observation signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) is below the corresponding diffusion SNR, and switches to a calibrated measurement-based predictor otherwise. We prove that the proposed sampler converges to the Bayesian posterior conditioned on the measurements. Empirical results show that the proposed sampler performs favorably against existing diffusion-based posterior samplers across a range of image restoration tasks, achieving the best performance on the majority of evaluation metrics considered. Overall, our results convert posterior sampling for noisy linear inverse problems to simple coordinate-wise DDIM updates, yielding an efficient, easy-to-implement algorithm with provable posterior consistency.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

### [122] [Predictive RTO for CoAP using Lightweight Support Vector Regression in Internet of Things](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18273)
*Tobias Hansson,Praveen Kumar Donta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出prCoAP，一种轻量级数据驱动方法，用线性SVR集成模型替代传统CoAP的启发式RTO选择，并加入随机森林丢弃分类器，在资源受限的IoT设备上实现自适应拥塞控制。实验表明，线性SVR在IEEE 802.15.4环境下达到97.25%的PDR，优于标准CoAP，且相比核SVR变体具有更低的能耗开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoAP现有的拥塞控制机制（如BEB、CoCoA/CoCoA+）依赖固定启发式方法，在动态和有损的无线链路环境下适应性差，无法高效处理IoT网络中的拥塞问题。

Method: 提出prCoAP框架：(1) 用每尝试次线性SVR集成模型，从节点可观测特征直接预测RTO；(2) 引入校准的随机森林丢弃分类器，识别可能失败的传输并提前终止；(3) 模型在低端微控制器上运行，满足严格内存和能耗约束。

Result: 线性SVR在离散事件仿真中达到97.25%的PDR，优于标准CoAP；核SVR的回归拟合更优（R² 0.84 vs 0.63），但线性SVR在系统级效率上更佳，在同等PDR下能耗更低。

Conclusion: prCoAP通过轻量级数据驱动方法有效替代了传统启发式拥塞控制，在保持高PDR的同时降低了能耗，适用于资源受限的IoT场景。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) networks require lightweight application layer messaging, and CoAP is an option because it supports REST-style interactions over UDP on constrained devices. However, CoAP congestion control still depends on fixed heuristics, including binary exponential backoff (BEB) and RTT-based mechanisms such as CoCoA and CoCoA+, which do not adapt well to dynamic and lossy wireless links. This paper proposes prCoAP, a lightweight data-driven approach that replaces heuristic Retransmission Timeout (RTO) selection with a per-attempt linear Support Vector Regression (SVR) ensemble for direct RTO prediction from node-observable features. The model runs on-device on low-end microcontrollers and operates within strict memory and energy budgets. The framework also includes a calibrated Random Forest drop classifier that identifies likely-to-fail transactions in later retransmission attempts and terminates them early to reduce channel occupancy. We evaluate the approach using a discrete-event simulator implementing IEEE 802.15.4 and RFC 7252 and validate it against the FIT IoT-LAB testbed. Our experiments confirm that the proposed linear SVR achieves 97.25% PDR, outperforming standard CoAP under the evaluated conditions. We also evaluate a kernel SVR variant; while it improves regression fit (R2 0.84 vs. 0.63), the linear SVR provides better system-level efficiency, achieving comparable PDR with lower energy overhead.

</details>


### [123] [What Governs Decode Throughput in Absolute-Offset GPU LZ77? A Work-Granularity Mechanism and an Encode-Time Min-Match-Length Lever](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18541)
*Yakiv Shavidze*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文揭示LZ77格式的解码吞吐量主要由平均匹配长度（工作粒度）决定，而非占用率、计算或地址分散等因素。通过提高最小匹配长度，可在不解码内核修改的情况下同时提升压缩率和解码吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACEAPEX系列工作建立了无损LZ77格式，支持GPU并行解码和亚毫秒级区域定位，但未解释解码吞吐量的决定因素及优化方法。本文旨在回答这两个问题。

Method: 在NVIDIA H100上进行控制消融实验，证明解码吞吐量由平均匹配长度（工作粒度）主导。通过合成拷贝核测试（匹配长度32-1024字节，吞吐量跨度3.5倍），并在真实数据上验证。提出编码端策略：按距离类提高最小匹配长度（从6/8/10/12提升至12/16/24/32）。

Result: FASTQ解码从142.6提升至178.6 GB/s（提升25%），压缩比改善1.8%；enwik9吞吐量提升78%。所有数据集同时获得压缩比和吞吐量提升，无一例外。合成测试显示平均匹配长度从32到1024字节时，吞吐量从212到744 GB/s（3.5倍跨度）。真实数据平均匹配长度较低（enwik9: 6.5, FASTQ: 10.1）。

Conclusion: 解码吞吐量受工作粒度（平均匹配长度）主导；提高最小匹配长度可同时改善压缩比和吞吐量，二者并非权衡关系，而是源于移除远端偏移的短匹配（其熵成本高于节省）。所有结果位精确可验证，且不声称超越硬件带宽上限。

Abstract: The ACEAPEX line of work established a lossless LZ77 format whose back-references are absolute output positions, giving parallel, compressed-resident GPU decode with sub-millisecond region seek. What it did not establish is what governs the decode throughput of such a format, or how to improve it. This paper answers both. Through controlled ablations on an NVIDIA H100 we show that decode throughput is governed not by occupancy, compute, address scatter, or launch parallelism, but by work granularity: throughput is a function of the average match length, because a short match leaves most lanes of a cooperating warp idle. A synthetic copy kernel confirms a 3.5x throughput span (212 to 744 GB/s) as average match length grows from 32 to 1024 bytes. Real data sit at the low end (mean match length 6.5 on enwik9, 10.1 on FASTQ). We then show that this mechanism yields a practical, encode-side lever: raising the minimum match length by distance class (6/8/10/12 to 12/16/24/32) improves both compression ratio and decode throughput simultaneously on all eight tested datasets, with no exceptions and no change to the decode kernel. FASTQ decode rises from 142.6 to 178.6 GB/s while ratio improves 1.8%; enwik9 throughput rises 78%. This is not a trade-off: both gains follow from one cause, removing short matches whose far offsets cost more entropy than they save. All figures are bit-perfect (FNV on GPU paths, byte compare on CPU paths) and git-verifiable. Scope is explicit: figures are match-phase, device-resident; entropy and host transfer are outside the timer; seek is read/block-level, not coordinate-level; and we do not claim to exceed the hardware bandwidth ceiling.

</details>


### [124] [Searching for Plans You Can Actually Build: A Realizability-Aware Full-Space Optimizer for MoE Training and Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18631)
*Quan Yuan,Jie Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: moefs是一个面向MoE训练和服务的可实现性感知全空间优化器，将部署可实现性作为首要搜索约束，通过三层搜索（并行度、调度、内核）生成可实际部署的Megatron训练栈和SGLang服务栈，并在两代硬件上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE优化器假设成本模型能排名的计划空间与实际工具链能构建的计划空间一致，导致返回的计划理论上最优但实际无法部署。需要一种能感知部署可实现性的全空间优化方法。

Method: 提出moefs，三层搜索：并行度、调度、内核；将部署可实现性作为一等约束；对实现开销进行定价而非禁止；从单一计划同时生成Megatron训练栈和SGLang服务栈；所有预测预注册并哈希锁定后执行实验。

Result: 2×RTX4090训练：优于最强手工基线0.9%（超出验收线2.9%）；8×H800服务：吞吐率1.0304匹配手工配置；8×H800训练：诚实报告FAIL（吞吐率0.9338），因单个调度标志失败。

Conclusion: moefs验证了将部署可实现性作为一等约束的有效性，能够诚实报告失败而非给出虚假最优，为MoE系统优化提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) systems split a program's plan space in two: the space a cost model can rank, and the smaller space a real toolchain can actually build. Automatic optimizers rank the first and silently assume the two coincide -- so they can return a plan that is optimal on paper and impossible to emit. We present moefs, a realizability-aware full-space optimizer for MoE training and serving that makes deployment realizability a first-class search constraint. moefs closes a three-tier search over parallelism, schedule, and kernels; it emits both a Megatron training stack and an SGLang serving stack from a single plan; and it prices, rather than forbids, the realization overheads it measures. We evaluate across two hardware generations. On 2x RTX4090, the searched training plan edges the strongest hand-tuned baseline by +0.9% (and clears the 0.98x acceptance bar by +2.9%); on 8x H800, the searched serving plan matches the hand-tuned configuration at a 1.0304 throughput ratio. We hold failures to the same standard: on 8x H800 training, the searched plan is a computable, honest FAIL at 0.9338 of the best hand-tuned throughput, losing on a single schedule flag. All predictions are pre-registered in a frozen, artifact-hashed adjudication file before the H800 runs, and every outcome is reported as-is.

</details>


### [125] [Unstructured Hydrodynamics on Spatial Dataflow Architectures: A Joint Code and Data Decomposition Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18650)
*Piotr Luczynski,Tal Ben-Nun,Leighton Wilson,Brian Van Essen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将非结构化网格问题映射到空间数据流架构（如Cerebras晶圆级引擎）上的方法，通过通信与内存建模、空间填充曲线降维及内存优化技术，成功将LULESH应用映射到该架构，并在性能上超越了GPU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间数据流架构在高性能计算中表现优异，但现有设计仅针对结构化网格问题进行优化；实际多物理场代码常使用非结构化网格，其间接内存访问和高维通信模式使其难以直接映射到这类架构上。因此需要一种系统化的方法来解耦非结构化问题。

Method: 采用以模型为中心的原则性方法：(1) 对原始代码进行自动化分析；(2) 定义高维分解，通过空间填充曲线最小化通信；(3) 应用内存优化技术（考虑到该架构内存受限的环境）；(4) 提出联合分解，同时考虑应用场的大小及其子程序。

Result: 将LULESH（Livermore非结构化拉格朗日显式冲击流体动力学）应用成功映射到Cerebras晶圆级引擎上，实验结果表明较大的非结构化网格代码仍能超越GPU性能。

Conclusion: 通过模型驱动的联合分解方法，非结构化网格问题可以有效映射到空间数据流架构，且性能优于GPU，表明这类架构在更广泛的科学计算场景中具有潜力。

Abstract: Spatial Dataflow Architectures are an emerging hardware pattern in high-performance computing, whose mesh-connected fixed-memory processing elements are tailored for structured grid kernels with two-dimensional neighborhoods. However, practical multiphysics codes are often computed on unstructured grids, which induce indirect memory accesses and high-dimensional communication patterns, making them infeasible to directly map onto said architectures. This work takes a principled, model-centric approach to partitioning unstructured problems onto spatial dataflow architectures. Through communication and memory modeling, we propose a joint decomposition that considers both the size of the application's fields and its subroutines. In particular, we automate the analysis process of the original code, define a high-dimensional decomposition that minimizes communication via space-filling curves, and apply memory optimization techniques, crucial in this memory-limited environment. We demonstrate mapping the Livermore Unstructured Lagrangian Explicit Shock Hydrodynamics (LULESH) application to the Cerebras Wafer-Scale Engine, showing that larger, unstructured grid codes can still outperform GPUs.

</details>


### [126] [Mapping Without Graphs: Learning Coherence Traffic for Task Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18879)
*Guochu Xiong,Tianrui Ma,Weichen Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CoTM，一种感知缓存一致性的任务映射框架，通过从动态一致性行为推断任务间依赖来构建任务图，采用轻量级启发式与多起点优化策略，相比现有方法减少最多47.85%的平均链路利用率和10.30%的总能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务映射方法依赖预定义任务图，仅从程序结构或运行时信息（如数据流、同步、profiling）提取依赖，未显式捕获由共享数据访问引发的一致性交互，且普遍忽略缓存一致性流量，导致建模与实际通信行为不匹配，映射次优、性能下降。

Method: CoTM框架：1）从动态缓存一致性行为推断任务间依赖，构建任务图；2）采用轻量级启发式算法结合多起点优化策略迭代精化任务放置；3）设计感知一致性的惩罚函数，联合优化一致性流量与NoC性能指标。

Result: 相比现有方法，平均链路利用率降低最高47.85%，总能耗降低最高10.30%。

Conclusion: 将缓存一致性纳入任务映射至关重要，感知一致性的优化对未来众核NoC系统具有显著潜力。

Abstract: Cache coherence is essential for communication in many-core Network-on-Chip (NoC)-based systems. As application scale and complexity increase, efficiently managing communication becomes increasingly challenging, making task mapping a key optimization technique. However, existing task mapping approaches suffer from two major limitations. First, they rely on predefined task graphs whose dependencies are typically derived from program structure or runtime information, such as dataflow, synchronization, traces, or profiling, without explicitly capturing coherence-induced interactions arising from shared data accesses. Consequently, these graphs provide only a partial representation of inter-task relationships, limiting mapping effectiveness. Second, they generally overlook cache coherence, even though coherence traffic constitutes a significant portion of NoC communication. This mismatch between modeled communication behavior and actual runtime interactions often leads to suboptimal mappings and degraded system performance. To address these limitations, we propose CoTM, a coherence-aware task mapping framework that constructs task graphs by inferring inter-task dependencies from dynamic coherence behavior. CoTM employs a lightweight heuristic with a multi-start optimization strategy to iteratively refine task placement, guided by a coherence-aware penalty function that jointly considers coherence traffic and NoC performance metrics. Experimental results demonstrate that CoTM reduces average link utilization by up to 47.85% and total energy consumption by up to 10.30% compared with existing approaches. These results highlight the importance of incorporating cache coherence into task mapping and demonstrate the potential of coherence-aware optimization for future many-core NoC systems.

</details>


### [127] [A User-oriented Portable, Reproducible, and Scalable Software Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18929)
*Alfio Lazzaro,Utz-Uwe Haus,Sandrine Charousset,Nina Mujkanovic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个统一的软件生态系统，允许科学家在不同硬件平台（从笔记本电脑到超算和云资源）上使用相同的开发环境运行工作流，通过模块化的统一命令行接口实现跨平台交互，并在EU H2020项目中成功验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学家通常在多样化的硬件环境（笔记本电脑、工作站、超算、云资源）上进行研究，标准场景需要混合使用这些资源，但缺乏统一的跨平台开发环境。

Method: 构建了一个模块化的统一命令行接口软件生态系统，使用户能够在不同计算资源上依赖相同的开发环境运行工作流。

Result: 该软件生态系统已在plan4res EU H2020项目中成功测试，并证明可扩展至其他有类似需求的项目。

Conclusion: 该统一软件生态系统实现了跨异构硬件平台的开发环境一致性，为计算工作流的执行提供了可复用的解决方案。

Abstract: It is normal for scientists to perform their research on a diverse set of hardware, ranging from laptops and workstations to supercomputers and cloud resources. The standard scenario requires a mix of these resources. In this paper we describe a software ecosystem that enables users to rely on the same development environment for running their workflows across the different computational resources. We describe a modular, unified command-line interface that allows for the interaction with a user-workflow across diverse hardware platform. The software ecosystem has been successfully tested as part of the plan4res EU H2020 project. It can be extended to other projects with similar requirements, so that they can benefit from the same approach for executing computational workflows.

</details>


### [128] [InstantInfer: Enabling Fast LLM Cold Start with Communicating Finite Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18957)
*Yitao Yuan,Yongchao He,Shaoke Fang,Wenfei Wu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于通信有限自动机（CFA）的抽象方法，用于系统分析LLM推理服务冷启动中的跨组件优化机会，并设计编程框架实现安全并发的组件程序重构。基于该框架重构vLLM的进程树创建、张量加载和模型切换，构建冷启动系统InstantInfer，实验表明最高实现7.2倍加速，且在多种GPU、负载和规模下表现鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理服务的冷启动严重影响用户体验，现有方案因顺序初始化和细粒度I/O请求导致效率低下。虽然程序重构可实现并发执行和I/O合并，但面对海量异构组件时容易出错且存在正确性风险。

Method: 提出CFA抽象以系统分析跨组件优化机会，设计编程框架保留原始顺序程序结构同时实现安全的并发组件执行，并证明程序重构的正确性。将框架应用于vLLM的三个关键冷启动阶段（进程树创建、张量加载、模型切换），构建InstantInfer系统。

Result: InstantInfer在LLM冷启动中实现最高7.2倍加速，并在不同GPU、工作负载和系统规模下展现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CFA抽象和编程框架能够系统性地挖掘跨组件并行化和I/O合并机会，在保证正确性的前提下显著加速LLM推理服务的冷启动过程。

Abstract: Cold starts in large language model (LLM) inference services significantly affect user experience, yet they remain inefficient due to sequential initialization and a massive number of fine-grained I/O requests issued by complex software components. Although refactoring the program can yield advantages such as concurrent execution and I/O merging, this approach is error-prone and carries correctness risks when dealing with massive, heterogeneous components. We propose the Communicating Finite Automata (CFA) abstraction to systematically analyze cross-component optimization opportunities, and design a programming framework to enable CFA-based component program refactoring. This framework preserves the original sequential program structure while enabling safe concurrent component execution. We prove the correctness of the program refactoring. We apply the CFA abstraction and framework to refactor process tree creation, tensor loading, and model switching in vLLM, forming a new cold-start system named InstantInfer. Extensive experiments demonstrate that InstantInfer substantially accelerates LLM cold starts (achieving up to 7.2 times speedup) and exhibits robustness across diverse GPUs, workloads, and scales.

</details>


### [129] [Enabling Multi-Dimensional Distributed Trace Comparison with Contrast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19102)
*Vaastav Anand,Rodrigo Fonseca,Jonathan Mace,Antoine Kaufmann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Contrast是一个多维比较轨迹分析系统，通过引入轨迹投影对象（TPO）实现轨迹的结构、时间、关键路径和语义属性的可合并表示，支持查询时动态构建任意比较集，并提供了基于关键路径的可视化界面SpectroViz和基于LLM的自然语言界面Parallax两种互补诊断接口。在DeathStarBench和Uber生产轨迹上的实验验证了其有效性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式轨迹诊断本质上是比较任务，但现有方法面临三个挑战：(1) 轨迹间的有用差异可能体现在多个维度（结构、时间、关键路径、语义），单一诊断接口无法有效捕获所有维度；(2) 相关维度和比较对象通常无法预先确定，操作员需要在形成假设过程中动态构建和调整比较集；(3) 缺乏统一的表示方式支持任意比较集的灵活构建。

Method: Contrast的核心创新是Trace Projection Object (TPO)，一种可合并的轨迹表示，能够捕获轨迹的结构、时间、关键路径和语义属性，同时支持在查询时高效构建任意比较集。系统将轨迹表示与比较语义分离，允许不同接口选择性推理特定维度。在此基础上实现了两个互补接口：SpectroViz（基于关键路径的可视化接口，用于定位执行差异）和Parallax（利用LLM的自然语言接口，用于生成轨迹差异的解释）。

Result: 在DeathStarBench的受控实验和Uber生产轨迹数据上的评估表明，Contrast能够有效地支持多维度的轨迹比较分析，其TPO表示支持高效的查询时比较集构建，两种互补接口在不同维度上提供了有效的诊断能力。

Conclusion: Contrast通过TPO实现了轨迹表示与比较语义的分离，克服了传统方法固定差异定义的局限性，支持操作员动态构建比较集并从多维度进行组合分析，为分布式轨迹诊断提供了一种更灵活、更全面的解决方案。

Abstract: Diagnosis using distributed traces is fundamentally a comparative task: operators seek to understand how an anomalous execution differs from expected behavior, how a deployment changes system execution, or how two individual executions differ. Trace comparison is challenging because useful differences between executions can manifest across multiple dimensions, and no single diagnostic interface is effective at capturing all of them. Moreover, the relevant dimensions and comparison populations are often not known a priori; operators construct and refine comparison sets dynamically as they develop hypotheses about system behavior.
  This paper presents Contrast, a system for multi-dimensional comparative trace analysis. Contrast introduces the Trace Projection Object (TPO), a mergeable representation that captures structural, temporal, critical-path, and semantic properties of trace populations while enabling efficient construction of arbitrary comparison sets at query time. Unlike approaches that define a fixed notion of trace difference, Contrast separates trace representation from comparison semantics, allowing diverse interfaces to selectively reason about specific dimensions. This separation enables the composition of complementary interfaces, allowing operators to combine insights from multiple dimensions for more effective diagnosis. We demonstrate this capability through two complementary interfaces: (i) SpectroViz, a critical-path-based visual interface for localizing execution differences; and (ii) Parallax, a natural language interface for generating explanations of trace differences using LLMs.
  We demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of Contrast through controlled experiments on traces from DeathStarBench and evaluation on production traces from Uber.

</details>


### [130] [A Scalable Pattern Mining Workflow for Interpretable Machine Log Analysis in High-Performance Computing Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19143)
*Shilpika Shilpika,Bethany Lusch,Eric Pershey,Carlo Graziani,Venkatram Vishwanath,Michael E. Papka*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合高级模式匹配与挖掘技术的HPC日志分析工作流，利用Aho-Corasick自动机存储频繁日志模式，结合系统层次与消息优先级提取模式序列，并与作业日志关联聚类，以自动检测频繁错误和故障事件，通过案例研究证明了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代超级计算机每日生成海量异构日志数据，传统分析技术难以处理其规模和文本复杂性，需要更复杂的工作流来提取模式、发现异常，从而预测系统故障和效率低下问题。

Method: 提出一个日志分析工作流：1) 结合高级模式匹配与挖掘技术；2) 系统性地识别日志消息中的频繁模式及模式序列；3) 将其存储在有限状态自动机（如Aho-Corasick自动机）中；4) 利用系统层次结构和消息优先级信息辅助模式提取；5) 将识别出的错误序列与作业日志进行关联聚类，揭示具有相似或相异错误签名的应用组。

Result: 通过汇总统计和超级计算机案例研究证明了该方法的有效性，表明模式挖掘对于释放日志数据潜力、实现实时分析以及构建更具弹性和可扩展性的HPC系统至关重要。

Conclusion: 模式挖掘是充分发挥HPC日志数据价值的关键，能够实现实时分析，帮助构建更具弹性和可扩展性的高性能计算系统。

Abstract: Modern supercomputers housed in High Performance Computing (HPC) environments generate massive volumes of log data daily, revealing intricate information and performance metrics about these complex systems. The sheer size and heterogeneous nature of HPC logs, especially text data, pose significant challenges for traditional analytical techniques. Consequently, more complex workflows are necessary for pattern extraction when analyzing these logs, enabling the discovery of underlying patterns and anomalies that may indicate system faults and help predict future failures and inefficiencies. Our log analysis workflow investigates a combination of advanced pattern-matching and mining techniques applied to HPC log analysis. By systematically identifying frequent log patterns and pattern sequences in log messages and storing them in a finite-state automaton, such as the Aho-Corasick automaton, our workflow enables automated detection of frequent errors and fault events. To extract these patterns and sequences, we leverage information about system hierarchy and message priority. We then correlate and cluster the identified error sequences with job logs, revealing groups of applications with similar or dissimilar error signatures. This approach yields insights that inform improvements and guide real-time monitoring efforts. Our research establishes that pattern mining is vital for unlocking the full potential of log data by enabling real-time analysis and contributing to more resilient, scalable HPC systems. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach through summary statistics and a case study on an exascale-class system supercomputer.

</details>


### [131] [Coherence in Control: Bridging Many-Core Mapping and Routing through Cost Unification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19158)
*Guochu Xiong,Xiangzhong Luo,Weichen Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo框架，通过统一代价模型联合优化任务映射和路由，在考虑缓存一致性的情况下，相比现有方法降低88.46%链路利用率、17.40%包延迟和17.58%执行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务映射和路由联合优化方法忽视了缓存一致性开销，导致优化目标与实际通信模式不匹配；同时使用分离的代价评估器导致决策冲突，无法捕捉一致性引发的两阶段耦合。

Method: CoCo框架包含统一代价模型（整合通信代价、一致性开销和负载不均衡），结合一致性引导的任务映射和基于强化学习的路由，通过根据通信行为调整定向链路权重来改善流量分布。

Result: 相比现有方法，链路利用率降低88.46%，包延迟降低17.40%，执行时间降低17.58%。

Conclusion: 缓存一致性在多核系统联合优化设计中至关重要，CoCo通过一致性感知的联合优化显著提升了系统性能。

Abstract: The rapid growth of data-intensive applications increases communication demands in many-core systems, where cache coherence, while essential for correct communication and data consistency, introduces substantial overhead due to frequent data sharing and coherence activities. As system scale and workload complexity grow, the resulting coherence traffic intensifies communication pressure, making the co-optimization of task mapping and routing essential for improving system performance. However, most existing approaches overlook cache coherence, leaving a substantial portion of coherence-induced communication unaccounted for and creating a mismatch between optimization objectives and actual communication patterns. Furthermore, by employing separate cost evaluators for mapping and routing, these approaches complicate objective coordination, may lead to conflicting decisions, and fail to capture the coherence-induced coupling between the two stages. To address these challenges, we propose CoCo, a coherence-aware co-optimization framework that jointly integrates task mapping and routing under a unified cost model for realistic scenarios. This unified model integrates communication cost, coherence overhead, and load imbalance into a single objective, enabling coherence-aware decision-making and effective trade-offs among optimization goals. Guided by this model, CoCo combines coherence-guided task mapping with reinforcement learning-based routing, where directional link weights are adjusted according to communication behavior to improve traffic distribution, enabling coherence-aware co-optimization for many-core systems. Experimental results show that CoCo reduces link utilization by 88.46%, packet delay by 17.40%, and execution time by 17.58% compared with existing approaches, highlighting the importance of cache coherence in co-optimization design.

</details>


### [132] [ARBITER: Guarded Agentic Control for SLO-Oriented Kubernetes Remediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19182)
*Pooyan Habibi,Alberto Leon-Garcia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ARBITER是一个基于OpenTelemetry的因果资源图和有限类型动作接口的Kubernetes SLO修复控制平面。它通过确定性规划器和LLM驱动的规划（如Sonnet）在安全基底（策略门、资源/扰动预算、审批和有限执行）下选择正确的修复动作和目标。在DeathStarBench和Online Boutique上的实验表明，ARBITER能可靠选择回滚（HPA失败）和定位下游关键路径故障，而资源自动缩放无法做到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Kubernetes自动伸缩器只能观察粗粒度的资源指标，最近的SLO控制器依赖自定义遥测，而无约束的智能体操作器无法安全地变更生产集群。因此需要一个既有SLO导向修复能力又具备安全保证的控制平面。

Method: ARBITER构建了一个OpenTelemetry原生的因果资源图，组装有界的DiagnosisContext对象，并暴露一个有限类型化动作接口来分离规划与执行。该接口既支持确定性规划器，也支持LLM驱动的规划器，并配合确定性模式检查、策略门、资源/扰动预算、审批和有界执行作为安全基底。

Result: 在4节点Kubernetes集群上，对于bad-image部署回归，ARBITER在10次CPU烧灼和纯延迟实验中均选择rollback_canary，而HPA要么扩展故障镜像要么从不触发。对于下游关键路径故障，确定性ARBITER和审批门控的Sonnet都能定位到home-timeline-service，而HPA/纯资源控制从未做到。额外实验验证了守卫式放置修复、可移植性、对抗性安全拒绝、离线多模型回放和KWOK控制平面扩展性。

Conclusion: ARBITER通过有限类型化动作接口和分层安全基底，成功地在Kubernetes上实现了安全且有效的SLO导向修复，弥补了资源自动缩放无法提供的修复动作选择和下游目标选择能力。代码和实验材料已开源。

Abstract: Maintaining service-level objectives (SLOs) on Kubernetes microservices remains difficult because autoscalers observe coarse resource metrics, recent SLO controllers often depend on custom telemetry, and unconstrained agentic operators cannot safely mutate production clusters. We present ARBITER, a guarded control plane for SLO-oriented Kubernetes remediation. ARBITER builds an OpenTelemetry-native causal resource graph, assembles bounded DiagnosisContext objects, and exposes a finite typed-action interface that separates planning from execution. The same interface supports deterministic planners and an LLM-backed planning harness, with deterministic schema checks, policy gates, resource/disruption budgets, approval, and bounded execution forming the safety substrate. We evaluate ARBITER on a 4-node Kubernetes cluster using DeathStarBench Social Network and Online Boutique. The evaluation tests two forms of SLO-oriented control that resource autoscaling alone does not provide: selecting the right remediation action and selecting the right downstream target. For bad-image deployment regressions, ARBITER selects rollback_canary in all ten CPU-burn and pure-latency runs; HPA either scales the faulty image or never triggers. For a downstream critical-path fault, the user-visible breach appears at the frontend, but trace evidence identifies home-timeline-service as the remediable bottleneck. Deterministic ARBITER and a live approval-gated Sonnet harness target that downstream service in every replicate, whereas HPA/resource-only control never does. Additional experiments cover guarded placement repair, Online Boutique portability, adversarial safety rejection, offline multi-model replay, and KWOK-based control-plane scale evidence. We release the controller, replay corpus, harnesses, safety tests, and figure artifacts: https://github.com/pooyan/arbiter.

</details>


### [133] [Keeping the Cache Warm Pays: Keepalive Economics for Agentic Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19214)
*Maxim Khailo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 大型语言模型（LLM）的提示缓存前缀在代理工作负载中因暂停时间过长而被逐出，导致成本增加。本文提出客户端保活机制，通过在暂停期间定时重播前缀来保持缓存热度，可降低后续请求成本最多12.5倍，并推导出最优保活间隔约为提供方TTL减去安全余量（如Anthropic的5分钟TTL下约4分钟）。论证了理性采用会导致普遍采用，进而使LRU驱逐失效，最终倒逼提供方转向按缓存驻留时间计费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理工作负载在发送请求后需要等待工具执行或用户批准，这种分钟级的暂停导致LLM提供方的提示缓存前缀被逐出，使得后续请求不得不重新支付完整的预填充成本和延迟，严重破坏了缓存带来的经济和效率优势。

Method: 提出客户端保活（keepalive）机制：在代理暂停期间，以一定时间间隔向提供方发送仅包含缓存前缀的轻量请求，防止LRU缓存将其逐出。通过理论推导和经济分析，研究保活间隔的最优选择，并在Anthropic、OpenAI、Google和DeepSeek等平台上验证效果。

Result: 保活机制可降低暂停后请求成本最多12.5倍。保活成本随间隔单调递减，最优间隔为略低于提供方TTL的最大值（如Anthropic的5分钟TTL下约4分钟），而非常见的30秒惯例。盈亏平衡点约为τ(w/r - 1)（Anthropic约46分钟，OpenAI和DeepSeek约36分钟）。

Conclusion: 保活机制对每个用户个体理性，普遍采用将导致缓存层被保活请求饱和，使LRU驱逐算法失效。这种外部性将推动提供方从按每次读取计费转向直接按缓存驻留时间（token-hour）计费，且已有提供方开始这样做。在提供方改变政策前，用户应采用本文推导的最优保活策略。

Abstract: Frontier LLM providers cache a prompt's processed prefix so that a follow-up request sharing it pays ~10% of the input price and skips most of the prefill latency. Agentic workloads systematically destroy this benefit: the agent sends a request, runs a tool or waits for approval for minutes, and by the time the follow-up is sent the cached prefix has been evicted, so the agent pays the full prefill again. A client-side keepalive, replaying the prefix on a timer during the pause, prevents this, and it is individually rational: across Anthropic, OpenAI, Google, and DeepSeek we show that a keepalive holds the prefix warm through gaps where idle baselines are evicted, cutting the post-pause request cost by up to 12.5x. The strategic question is the ping frequency, and it has a clean answer: keepalive cost falls monotonically in the interval, so the economical choice is the largest interval safely under the provider's TTL, about 4 minutes at Anthropic's 5-minute TTL rather than the 30-second convention, and the strategy breaks even against a re-prefill at idle ~tau(w/r - 1) (~46 min for Anthropic, ~36 min for OpenAI and DeepSeek). Because the benefit is real and bounded only by each user's own bill, rational adoption is universal adoption; and since cache residency is priced per read rather than per token-hour, a keepalive-saturated tier gives LRU eviction nothing to rank. We argue this externality will push providers to meter cache residency directly, and one already does. We derive the operator's policy until then.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [SysAdmin: Measuring Instrumental Power-Seeking in Frontier AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18239)
*Mana Azarm,Qiyao Wei,Rahul Nambiar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SysAdmin基准测试，用于评估前沿语言模型在自治系统管理环境中寻求权力的倾向（包括自我保护、增加自主性、资源获取、环境修改和策略性隐藏五个维度）。通过对7个前沿模型在2800个任务中进行评估，经偏差校正后，各模型的自发权力寻求行为约为0-5%。同时通过显式权力寻求提示的阳性对照验证了测量灵敏度。研究发现当前前沿模型在自然系统管理情境中表现出极少的自发权力寻求行为，但发现了比权力寻求更明显的其他故障模式，如规格游戏和抵抗目标修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权力寻求行为被识别为导致失控(LoC)风险的关键驱动因素，但目前缺乏系统性的基准测试来衡量AI系统的权力寻求倾向。

Method: 构建SysAdmin基准测试，将前沿语言模型定位为高保真Linux沙箱中的自治系统管理员，测量五个维度的权力寻求倾向。评估7个前沿模型在4种实验条件下的2800个任务，并使用人工标注的校准数据进行偏差校正。

Result: 偏差校正后的权力寻求估计值为每个模型0-5%；显式权力寻求提示的阳性对照达到100%检测率；发现了规格游戏和抵抗目标修改等更明显的故障模式。

Conclusion: 当前前沿模型在自然系统管理情境中表现出极少的自发权力寻求行为，但评估必须测试多样化的错位模式，因为存在模型特定的故障模式。

Abstract: Power-seeking defined as behaviors where AI systems acquire resources, evade oversight, or resist termination beyond task requirements is identified as a key driver of Loss of Control (LoC) risk. In this work, we introduce SysAdmin, a benchmark that positions frontier language models as autonomous system administrators in a high-fidelity Linux sandbox to measure power-seeking propensity across five dimensions: self-preservation, increasing autonomy, resource acquisition, environment modification, and strategic concealment. We evaluated seven frontier models across four experimental conditions in a total of 2800 tasks.
